## Consequential mathematical readings

- Scan 125, L3; Corollaire 3, crowded second determinant and right edge: |\det(1+A+B)|\leq\tfrac12\bigl(\det(1+|A|+|B|)+\det(1+|A^{\unsure{*}}|^{\unsure{\prime|,}}+|B^{\unsure{*}}|') — Retains possible adjoint marks on both operators in the second determinant; H removes those marks. Their identities remain uncertain.
- Scan 125, L13; determinant identity variables: \det(1+\unsure{R|h}+k)=\det(1+h)\bigl(1+\tfrac{h}{1+h}\bigr)=\det(1+h)\det(1+Hk) — The numerator is the written h, while the later product is Hk; these are not silently made algebraically consistent. The initial variable remains R/h doubtful.
- Scan 125, L14; definition of H: H=\frac{1}{1+h} — Uses the written lower-case h in the reciprocal defining H, linking it to the reduction’s h rather than H’s L.

- Scan 125, L16; alpha comparison: \alpha_n(Hk)\leq\alpha_n(k) — Keeps k as the operand of both alpha expressions, consistently with the immediately preceding determinant comparison.
